How to read this log
  The reference results are the original Python runs on Fedora; the rerun used the container on
  Linux. Results are not expected to be identical to the last digit across operating
  systems and linear algebra libraries, and statistics based on random subsamples,
  permutations or nearest neighbours can change more. Each table is given one result:
    SAME                      all numbers within 1e-6 (relative), text identical
    SAME UP TO SIGN           only opposite signs of equal size (eigenvector signs)
    SAME VALUES, OTHER ORDER  the same rows in a different order
    TEXT ONLY                 numbers the same, some text differs
    WITHIN 0.1%               numbers differ by more than 1e-6 but at most 0.1%
    OUTSIDE 0.1%              some numbers differ by more than 0.1%
  Tables only in the reference come from scripts or steps that are not part of the
  deposit. Known causes are given as notes under the entries.
